Let's design concrete algorithm that is robust and not too long.

Available libraries: numpy, scipy. We can implement our own objective fast:

def objective_fast(a):
  a=np.asarray(a,float); a=np.clip(a,0,1000); n=a.size; if n==0 or sum<0.01 return inf
  conv=fullconv(a); M=conv.max(); S=conv.sum? Actually sum_a=a.sum(); return 2*n*M/S*S.
Full convolution via FFT for n> maybe 512 else np.convolve.
Use scipy.signal.fftconvolve? We can implement with np.fft.rfft. For real arrays, length m = next_fast_len(2*n-1). Use scipy.fftpack.next_fast_len if available; else power of 2. m=1; while m<2n-1: m*=2 (fast enough). For n up to 20000, m=65536. Use:
fa=np.fft.rfft(a[:n], m); ca=fa*fa; c=np.fft.irfft(ca, m)[:2*n-1]; due numerical small negative; M=max(c).
For exact security, final verify with evaluate_sequence if finite.

Multiplicative active-diagonal local search:
Given a >0 (avoid zeros with eps). Compute conv c. M=max. Choose active indices where c_k >= M*(1 - tol) or top K. Weights w = normalized active weights maybe (c_k-M*0.5)_+.
Compute influence d_i = ∑_{k active} w_k * 2 a_i a_{k-i} if valid. Can do efficiently: For each active k, perform add to d at i for valid i: a_i * a_{k-i}. Since active K small (<=64), loop K and vector slices. Let's derive slices for c_k=∑_{i=lo}^{hi} a_i a_{k-i}. For each i in [lo,hi], product p_i=a_i*a_{k-i}. We need d_i += 2*w*p_i. For derivative log. We can do:
lo=max(0,k-(n-1)); hi=min(n-1,k)
i=np.arange(lo,hi+1); prod=a[i]*a[k-i]; d[lo:hi+1] += 2w*prod.
Complexities: K*n per iter; if n=2000, K=16 =>32k, ok. If n=50000, K=16 =>800k per iter, maybe okay for seconds? We can limit n for search to ~4000/10000. Then local iterations thousands.

Update choice: d_i is gradient of active M in log scale. To reduce M and keep sum, we can do multiplicative:
a *= exp(-step*(d_i - alpha)), where alpha chosen such that ∑ a_i exp(-step*d_i) ≈ S? If after update we normalize, scale irrelevant, so alpha not necessary? Multiplying all by common factor doesn't change normalized score. But shape change depends on relative d. If we just a *= exp(-η d), variables in hot diagonals decrease more; then normalize. This preserves positivity. But if d has global mean, it also scales shape? Normalization removes common factor, so effectively use d_i - mean? Since a*exp(-ηd)/sum: common factor exp(-η mean) cancel. So use mean of d weighted by a? To minimize change in S? We can set d_centered = d - np.average(d, weights=a) maybe. Then update a *= exp(-η*d_centered), normalize.
Alternatively, additive gradient with projection might allow zeros, but multiplicative stable.

Need choose active set: top B diagonals by c_k, but many plateaus (power law continuous) top all first half have equal M; using top B arbitrary may bias. - n=a.size

- cosnmax consecutive fail

```
import numpy as np
import time
import math

_EPS = 1e-12

def _pow2_len(m):
    if m <= 0: return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()

def _fft_conv_full(x):
    x=np.asarray(x,float)
    n=x.size
    if n == 0: return np.array([])
    if n < 128:
        return np.convolve(x,x, mode='full')
    m=_pow2_len(2*n-1)
    fx=np.fft.rfft(x,m)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fx*fx,m)[:2*n-1]
    # remove neg small
    return c

def _fast_score(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.inf
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)): return np.inf
    a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
    S=float(a.sum())
    if S <= 1e-6: return np.inf
    c=_fft_conv_full(a)
    if c.size==0: return np.inf
    M=float(np.max(c))
    if M <=0: return np.inf
    return float(2.0*n*M/(S*S))
```

def _local_search(a0, rng, time_limit, verbose=False):
    a = np.asarray(a0,float).copy()
    a = np.clip(a, _EPS, None)
    S=a.sum()
    if S <= 0: a=np.ones(a.size); S=1
    a = a/S
    n=a.size
    best=a.copy(); best_score=_fast_score(best)
    if not finite: return uniform...
    L=_pow2_len(max(2*n-1, 3*n-2))  # for influence; maybe too big. For influence we only need L>=3n-2; for score use same L? Use same to reuse FA. For score c from FA length L. If L=next_pow2(3n-2), okay.
    p=np.log(a)
    eta=0.12
    noise=0.02
    fail=0

    max_iter=100000
    while time.monotonic()-start < time_limit and iter<max_iter:

        M=float(c.max())

        # weights active

        if np.sum(w) <=0:

        w = w / w.sum()
        FW=np.fft.rfft(w,L)
        corr=np.fft.irfft(FA*np.conj(FW), L)[:n]
        d=2.0*a*corr
        mu=np.mean(d)
        d -= mu
        scale=float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
        if scale <= 1e-15: break
        d /= scale
        improved=False
        # line search
        for trial in range(4):
            et = eta * (0.5**trial)
            p_try = p - et*d
            if noise > 1e-6:

            a_try = np.exp(p_try)
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(a_try)): continue
            Stry=a_try.sum()
            if Stry <=0: continue
            a_try /= Stry
            sc = 2*n*_fast_conv_max(a_try, L?)

```
def _block_perturb_log(p, rng, block_frac=0.05, sigma=0.1):
    n=p.size
    w=int(max(1,n*block_frac)); start=rng.integers(0,n-w)
    p2=p.copy(); p2[start:start+w] += rng.normal(0,sigma)
    a=np.exp(np.clip(p2,-30,30)); a/=a.sum(); return a
```

def _make_power_law(n, p, offset, tail_q=0.0, ground=0.0):
    i=np.arange(n,dtype=float)
    a=np.power(i+offset, -p)
    if tail_q >0:
       t=i/float(max(1,n-1))
       a *= np.clip(1.0 - t**tail_q, 0.0, 1.0)  # if tail_q=0 skip
    # optional random low-freq? not here
    return a
```

def _make_recurrence(n, a0=1.0, target=1.0):
    a=np.zeros(n); a[0]=a0
    # for i=1: a[1]=target/(2*a0) if i<n
    for i in range(1,n):
       # c_i = sum_{j=0}^i a_j a_{i-j}; since a_i a_{i-i}=a_i²? Wait full convolution c_i for i<n-1 includes a_i² term. Recurrence should solve a_i? c_i includes 2 a_j a_{i-j} for j<i and a_i² if i=2j? Actually a_i appears in c_i as a_i^2 only if 2i=i? no j=i gives a_i a_0? Wait c_i =∑_{j=0}^i a_j a_{i-j}.        s=0
       for j in 1..i-1: s += a_j a_{i-j}
       coeff if i? For i>0, c_i = a0*a_i + a_i*a0 + ∑_{j=1}^{i-1} a_j a_{i-j} =2a0 a_i + s.
       Set = target => a_i=(target-s)/(2a0)
       if a_i<0: set 0? Break? For recurrence binomial a0=target? target=1, a0=1 gives positive. For A<1, positive maybe.
```

Block perturbation when fail:
```
def _block_perturb(a, rng, frac=0.05, sigma=0.2):
   p=np.log(np.clip(a,eps,None))
   w=int(frac*n)
   start=rng.integers(0,n-w)
   p[start:start+w] += rng.normal(0,sigma)
   a2=np.exp(np.clip(p,-30,30)); a2/=a2.sum(); return a2
```
If score improve, use as new a.

```
def propose_candidate(**kwargs):
    seed = int(kwargs.get('seed', 42))
    budget_s = float(kwargs.get('budget_s', 2400.0))

Implement `_fast_score` with L option:
```
def _fast_score(a, L=None):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0: return np.inf
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)): return np.inf
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    S = float(a.sum())
    if S <= 1e-8: return np.inf
    a = a / S
    if L is None:
        L = _pow2_len(2*n - 1)
    if L < 2*n - 1: L = _pow2_len(2*n - 1)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, L)[:2*n-1]
    M = float(np.max(c))
    if M <= 0 or not np.isfinite(M): return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * n * M)
```
But if L passed from local (>=3n), fine. For raw candidates not normalized, scaling invariant. For n small, if L large? no.

`_block_perturb_p(p, rng, frac, sigma)`:
```
def _block_perturb_p(p, rng, frac=0.05, sigma=0.15):
   n=p.size
   w=max(1,int(n*frac))
   start=int(rng.integers(0, max(1,n-w+1)))
   p2=p.copy()
   p2[start:start+w] += rng.normal(0.0, sigma, size=start+w-start)
   p2=np.clip(p2,-30,30)
   a=np.exp(p2)
   S=a.sum()
   if S <=0: return p
   a/=S
   return np.log(a)
```

```

Now, local search code details:
```
def _local_search(a0, rng, time_limit, verbose=False):
    a = np.asarray(a0,float).copy()
    a = np.clip(a, 1e-12, None)
    S=a.sum()
    if S<=0 or not finite:
       n=a.size if a.size>0 else 512
       a=np.ones(n)
    else: a=a/S
    n=a.size
    best=a.copy(); best_score=_fast_score(a)
    if not finite: return best, np.inf
    start=time.monotonic()
    limit=start+max(0.0,time_limit)
    L=_pow2_len(max(1,3*n-2))
    p=_p_to_a? no p=np.log(best)
    eta=0.15; noise=0.02; fail=0; it=0; max_iter=100000
    print start score?
    while time.monotonic() < limit - 1.0 and it < max_iter:
        it += 1
        fa=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
        c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,L)[:2*n-1]
        c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
        M=float(np.max(c))
        cur=2.0*n*M
        if cur + 1e-12 < best_score:
           best_score=cur; best=a.copy()
        # weights
        margin=0.05
        threshold=M*(1.0-margin)
        active=np.nonzero(c >= threshold)[0]
        if active.size ==0:
           # top 16
           k=min(16,2*n-1)
           active=np.argsort(c)[-k:][::-1]
        w=np.zeros(2*n-1)
        # To avoid too many active? if active.size > 4096 and n > 4096? maybe sample 4096 largest.
        if active.size > 8192:
           # get top 8192 by c
           top_idx = np.argpartition(c, -8192)[-8192:][::-1]? But active maybe. Simpler: active = np.argpartition(c, -8192)[::-1][:8192] if len>8192.
        w[active] = np.ones(active.size) # or c[active]
        wsum=w.sum()
        if wsum<=0: active all top16
        w/=wsum
        fw=np.fft.rfft(w,L)
        corr=np.fft.irfft(fa*np.conj(fw),L)[:n]
        d=2.0*a*corr
        dmean=float(np.mean(d))
        d=d-dmean
        scale=float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
        if scale <= 1e-18: break
        d_scaled=d/scale
        improved=False
        for trial in range(4):
            et=eta*(0.5**trial)
            p_try=p - et*d_scaled
            if noise > 1e-7:
                p_try = p_try + rng.normal(0.0, noise, n)
            a_try=_p_to_a(p_try)
            sc=_fast_score(a_try, L)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc + 1e-12 < best_score:
               best_score=sc; best=a_try.copy(); a=a_try; p=np.log(a); improved=True
               break
        if improved:
           eta=min(0.6, eta*1.05)
           noise=max(0.0, noise*0.98)
           fail=0
        else:
           eta=max(0.01, eta*0.75)
           fail +=1
           if fail % 20 ==0:
               noise=min(0.2, noise*1.5 + 0.01)
               p_try=_block_perturb_p(p,rng,0.08,0.2)
               a_try=_p_to_a(p_try)
               sc=_fast_score(a_try,L)
               if sc +1e-12 < best_score:
                   best_score=sc; best=a_try.copy(); a=a_try; p=p_try
          if verbose and it%50==0: print(...)
    return best,best_score
```

Now, final candidate generation and local allocations. Let's code `propose_candidate`:
```
def propose_candidate(**kwargs):
    seed = int(kwargs.get('seed', 42))
    budget_s = float(kwargs.get('budget_s', 2400.0))
    if budget_s <= 0: return [1.0]
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    t0=time.monotonic()
    if budget_s > 500: reserve=150.0
    elif budget_s > 150: reserve=60.0
    else: reserve=10.0
    deadline=t0+max(0.0, budget_s-reserve)
    store=[]
    # add binomial initial
    n_list=[2048,4096]
    if budget_s>1000: n_list=[1024,2048,4096,6144?] maybe 6144 not power; FFT okay. Use [1024,2048,4096]
    for n in n_list:
        b=_make_binomial(n)
        sc=_fast_score(b)
        _add_candidate(store,b,sc)
        # binomial with polynomial modulation
        for alpha in [-0.3,0.0,0.3,0.6]:
           i=np.arange(n,float); t=i/max(1,n-1); mod=1+alpha*(t-0.5); mod=np.clip(mod,1e-3,10)
           cand=b*mod; sc=_fast_score(cand); _add
        for alpha in [-0.3,0.3]:
           for f in [1,2,3]:
              # ensure nonnegative
              cand=b*(1+0.2*alpha? sin)
    # power law maybe
    for n in [2048,4096]:
       for p in [0.45,0.5,0.55]: ...
    # parent
    try: parent=height_sequence_1 ...
    # sort store
    store.sort(key=_store_key)
    # select top 5 unique by fast score
    selected=[]
    for sc,a in store:
       if len(selected)>=5: break
       # skip if same size and close? Not necessary
       selected.append((sc,a.copy()))
    # local search
    if selected:
       total_time = deadline - time.monotonic()
       if total_time >0:
          fractions=[0.4,0.3,0.15,0.1,0.05]
          for i,(sc,a) in enumerate(selected):
              rem=deadline-time.monotonic()
              if rem < 20: break
              frac=fractions[i] if i<len(fractions) else 0.05
              lim=min(rem*frac*1.2, rem-30) ensure >=1
              # if best current already <1.5 and i>0, reduce lim
              if best_score<1.5 and i>0: lim*=0.3
              info = _local_search(a, rng, lim, verbose=True)
              _add_candidate(store, info[0], info[1])
    # if time and best fast >1.5, try larger n
    store.sort(key=_store_key)
    best_score=store[0][0]; best_a=store[0][1]
    if time.monotonic() < deadline-60 and best_a.size < 8192 and best_score > 1.49:
       rem=deadline-time.monotonic()
       n_big=8192
       a_big=_resample_sequence(best_a, n_big)
       # also fresh binomial 8192
       b_big=_make_binomial(n_big); sc_b=_fast_score(b_big)
       if sc_b < _fast_score(a_big): a_big=b_big
       lim=min(rem-20, 300.0)
       if lim>30:
          a_big2, sc_big2 = _local_search(a_big, rng, lim, verbose=True)
          _add_candidate(store,a_big2, sc_big2)
    # exact verification
    store.sort(key=_store_key)
    final_candidates=[]
    for sc,a in store[:5]: final_candidates.append((sc,a))
    best_list = [float(x) for x in best_a]
    best_exact = None
    max_exact = 3 if len<=2048 else 2
    # to save time, evaluate best fast first
    # But exact evaluate_sequence may be slow for n=8192. If budget reserve 150, evaluate 1-2.
    for sc,a in final_candidates:
        if n too large and exact eval count > max: break
        lst=[float(x) for x in np.clip(a,0,1000)]
        try: exc=evaluate_sequence(lst)
        except Exception: exc=np.inf
        if np.isfinite(exc): best_exact=exc; best_list=lst
    # If exact not finite, fallback use best fast clipped
    return best_list

```python
import time
import numpy as np


DOCSTRING = """
Search for non-negative step-function heights minimizing:
    2 * n * max(full_convolution(a, a)) / (sum(a)**2)

Strategy:
- Use several informed parametric initializers:
  * truncated Coulomb/binomial-type sequences (coefficients of (1-z)^(-1/2))
  * power-law and alpha-sequence families
  * low-frequency polynomial/sinusoidal modulations of the Coulomb sequence
- Run a log-domain gradient / Muller-style descent using FFT-based gradients.
  The gradient is formed from a soft active set of near-maximum convolution
  diagonals and uses the scale-invariant log objective.
- Periodically perturb to escape basins, then resample to a larger length if
  time remains.
- Finally verify a few top candidates with the authoritative evaluate_sequence.
"""


def _store_key(item):
    return item[0]


def _pow2_len(m):
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def _fast_score(a, L=None):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
        return np.inf

    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    S = float(a.sum())
    if S <= 1e-9:
        return np.inf
    a = a / S

    if L is None:
        L = _pow2_len(2 * n - 1)
    if L < 2 * n - 1:
        L = _pow2_len(2 * n - 1)

    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, L)[:2 * n - 1]
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    M = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * n * M)


def _p_to_a(p):
    p = np.clip(np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64), -30.0, 30.0)
    a = np.exp(p)
    S = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(S) or S <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(p.size, dtype=np.float64) / max(1, p.size)
    return a / S


def _make_binomial(n):
    n = int(max(1, n))
    a = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a[0] = 1.0
    for i in range(1, n):
        a[i] = a[i - 1] * (2.0 * i - 1.0) / (2.0 * i)
    return a


def _make_alpha_sequence(n, alpha):
    n = int(max(1, n))
    a = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a[0] = 1.0
    for i in range(1, n):
        a[i] = a[i - 1] * (float(alpha) + i - 1.0) / float(i)
    return np.clip(a, 1e-12, None)


def _make_power_law(n, p=0.5, offset=1.0, tail_amp=0.0, tail_q=1.0):
    n = int(max(1, n))
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.power(i + float(offset), -float(p))
    if tail_amp > 0.0 and tail_q > 0.0 and n > 1:
        t = i / float(n - 1)
        a *= np.clip(1.0 - float(tail_amp) * np.power(t, float(tail_q)), 0.0, 1.0)
    return np.clip(a, 1e-12, None)


def _resample_sequence(a, n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(max(1, n))
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == n:
        out = a.copy()
    elif a.size == 1:
        out = np.full(n, float(a[0]), dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, a.size)
        x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
        out = np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
    out = np.clip(out, 0.0, 1000.0)
    S = float(out.sum())
    if S > 0.0 and np.isfinite(S):
        out = out / S
    else:
        out = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    return out


def _block_perturb_p(p, rng, frac=0.08, sigma=0.15):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    n = p.size
    if n <= 2:
        return p.copy()
    w = int(max(1, n * float(frac)))
    hi = max(2, n - w + 1)
    st = int(rng.integers(0, hi))
    np2 = p.copy()
    np2[st:st + w] += rng.normal(0.0, float(sigma), size=w)
    return np2


def _local_search(a0, rng, time_limit, verbose=False):
    a = np.asarray(a0, dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        n = 256
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.clip(a, 1e-12, None)
    S = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(S) or S <= 0.0:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        a = a / S

    best = a.copy()
    best_score = _fast_score(a)
    if not np.isfinite(best_score):
        return best, np.inf

    start = time.monotonic()
    limit = start + max(0.0, float(time_limit))
    L = _pow2_len(max(1, 3 * n - 2))
    p = np.log(best)

    eta = 0.10
    noise = 0.004
    fail = 0
    it = 0
    max_iter = 1000000

    if verbose:
        print(f"[local] start n={n} score={best_score:.10f}", flush=True)

    while time.monotonic() < max(start, limit) - 0.5 and it < max_iter:
        it += 1

        fa = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
        c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, L)[:2 * n - 1]
        c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(c)):
            break

        M = float(np.max(c))
        if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
            break
        cur = float(2.0 * n * M)
        if cur + 1e-12 < best_score:
            best_score = cur
            best = a.copy()

        margin = 0.05
        threshold = M * (1.0 - margin)
        active = np.nonzero(c >= threshold)[0]
        if active.size == 0:
            k = min(16, 2 * n - 1)
            active = np.argsort(c)[-k:][::-1]

        if active.size > 8192 and (2 * n - 1) > 8192:
            active = np.argpartition(c, -8192)[-8192:]

        w = np.zeros(2 * n - 1, dtype=np.float64)
        vals = c[active] - threshold
        if vals.size > 0 and np.any(vals > 0.0):
            w[active] = vals * vals
        else:
            w[active] = 1.0

        wsum = float(w.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(wsum) or wsum <= 0.0:
            k = min(16, 2 * n - 1)
            w = np.zeros(2 * n - 1, dtype=np.float64)
            top = np.argsort(c)[-k:][::-1]
            w[top] = 1.0
            wsum = float(w.sum())
            if wsum <= 0.0:
                break
        w /= wsum

        E = float(np.dot(w, c))
        if not np.isfinite(E) or E <= 0.0:
            E = M

        fw = np.fft.rfft(w, L)
        corr = np.fft.irfft(fa * np.conj(fw), L)[:n]
        d = 2.0 * a * corr

        g = d - 2.0 * E * a
        g = g - float(np.mean(g))
        scale = float(np.max(np.abs(g)))
        if not np.isfinite(scale) or scale <= 1e-18:
            break
        g = g / scale

        improved = False
        for trial in range(3):
            et = eta * (0.5 ** trial)
            p_try = p - et * g
            if noise > 1e-8:
                p_try = p_try + rng.normal(0.0, noise, size=n)
            a_try = _p_to_a(p_try)
            sc = _fast_score(a_try, L)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc + 1e-12 < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best = a_try.copy()
                a = a_try
                p = np.log(a)
                improved = True
                break

        if improved:
            eta = min(0.45, eta * 1.06)
            noise = max(0.0, noise * 0.98)
            fail = 0
        else:
            eta = max(0.005, eta * 0.72)
            fail += 1
            if fail % 25 == 0:
                noise = min(0.18, noise * 1.6 + 0.004)
                p_try = _block_perturb_p(p, rng, 0.08, 0.18)
                a_try = _p_to_a(p_try)
                sc = _fast_score(a_try, L)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc + 1e-12 < best_score:
                    best_score = sc
                    best = a_try.copy()
                    a = a_try
                    p = np.log(a)

        if verbose and it % 75 == 0:
            print(
                f"[local] n={n} iter={it} score={best_score:.10f} "
                f"eta={eta:.4f} noise={noise:.5f}",
                flush=True
            )

    return best, best_score


def _add_candidate(store, a, score):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        return
    store.append((float(score), a.copy()))
    store.sort(key=_store_key)
    while len(store) > 12:
        store.pop()


def _seed_candidates(store, rng, deadline):
    n_list = [1024, 2048, 4096]
    if deadline - time.monotonic() < 120.0:
        n_list = [512, 1024]

    t0 = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, 1024)

    for n in n_list:
        base = _make_binomial(n)
        _add_candidate(store, base, _fast_score(base))

        for alpha in (-0.35, -0.15, 0.0, 0.15, 0.35, 0.60):
            tt = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
            mod = 1.0 + alpha * (tt - 0.5)
            if np.min(mod) <= 1e-6:
                continue
            cand = base * mod
            _add_candidate(store, cand, _fast_score(cand))

        for gamma in (-0.30, 0.0, 0.30):
            tt = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
            mod = 1.0 + gamma * (tt * tt - 0.5)
            if np.min(mod) <= 1e-6:
                continue
            cand = base * mod
            _add_candidate(store, cand, _fast_score(cand))

        for freq in (1, 2, 3):
            tt = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
            for amp in (0.08, 0.15):
                cand = base * (1.0 + amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * freq * tt))
                if np.min(cand) > 0.0:
                    _add_candidate(store, cand, _fast_score(cand))
                cand = base * (1.0 + amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * freq * tt))
                if np.min(cand) > 0.0:
                    _add_candidate(store, cand, _fast_score(cand))

        for alpha in (0.25, 0.40, 0.50, 0.65):
            cand = _make_alpha_sequence(n, alpha)
            _add_candidate(store, cand, _fast_score(cand))

        for p in (0.45, 0.50, 0.55):
            for off in (1.0, 2.0):
                cand = _make_power_law(n, p=p, offset=off)
                _add_candidate(store, cand, _fast_score(cand))

    parent = None
    try:
        parent = height_sequence_1  # noqa: F821
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is not None:
        try:
            parr = np.asarray(parent, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            if parr.size > 1:
                for target_n in (2048, 4096):
                    cand = _resample_sequence(parr, target_n)
                    _add_candidate(store, cand, _fast_score(cand))
                cand = parr
                if cand.size >= 256 and cand.size <= 8192:
                    _add_candidate(store, cand, _fast_score(cand))
        except Exception:
            pass

    if not store:
        a = _make_binomial(1024)
        _add_candidate(store, a, _fast_score(a))


def propose_candidate(**kwargs):
    seed = int(kwargs.get("seed", 42))
    try:
        budget_s = float(kwargs.get("budget_s", 2400.0))
    except Exception:
        budget_s = 2400.0

    if budget_s <= 0.0:
        return [1.0]

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    t0 = time.monotonic()

    if budget_s > 600.0:
        reserve_s = 180.0
    elif budget_s > 150.0:
        reserve_s = 60.0
    else:
        reserve_s = 10.0

    deadline = t0 + max(0.0, budget_s - reserve_s)
    store = []

    _seed_candidates(store, rng, deadline)
    store.sort(key=_store_key)

    if store:
        top = store[0]
        print(f"[init] best fast score={top[0]:.10f} n={top[1].size}", flush=True)

    selected = []
    for sc, a in store:
        if len(selected) >= 5:
            break
        selected.append((float(sc), a.copy()))

    fractions = (0.42, 0.28, 0.16, 0.09, 0.05)
    for idx, (sc, a) in enumerate(selected):
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 25.0:
            break
        frac = fractions[idx] if idx < len(fractions) else 0.05
        lim = rem * frac * 1.25
        lim = max(5.0, min(lim, rem - 20.0))
        if sc < 1.52 and idx > 0:
            lim *= 0.35

        print(f"[search] variant {idx} fast={sc:.10f} n={a.size} time={lim:.1f}s", flush=True)
        out, out_score = _local_search(a, rng, lim, verbose=True)
        _add_candidate(store, out, out_score)

    store.sort(key=_store_key)
    if store:
        print(f"[after local] best fast score={store[0][0]:.10f} n={store[0][1].size}", flush=True)

    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 90.0 and store and store[0][1].size < 8192 and store[0][0] > 1.498:
        base_big = _resample_sequence(store[0][1], 8192)
        fresh_big = _make_binomial(8192)
        sc_base = _fast_score(base_big)
        sc_fresh = _fast_score(fresh_big)
        big_seed = base_big if sc_base <= sc_fresh else fresh_big
        lim = min(rem - 25.0, 420.0)
        if lim > 20.0:
            print(f"[upsample] n=8192 fast={ _fast_score(big_seed):.10f } time={lim:.1f}s", flush=True)
            out, out_score = _local_search(big_seed, rng, lim, verbose=True)
            _add_candidate(store, out, out_score)

    store.sort(key=_store_key)
    if not store:
        fallback = _make_binomial(1024)
        return [float(x) for x in fallback / fallback.sum()]

    best_score, best_a = store[0]

    if best_a.size > 6000:
        eval_limit = 1
    elif best_a.size > 3000:
        eval_limit = 2
    else:
        eval_limit = 3

    best_list = [float(x) for x in np.clip(best_a, 0.0, 1000.0)]
    evaluated = 0

    for sc, a in store[:eval_limit]:
        if best_a.size > 6000 and evaluated >= 1:
            break
        if best_a.size <= 6000 and evaluated >= eval_limit:
            break
        lst = [float(x) for x in np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)]
        try:
            exc = float(evaluate_sequence(lst))  # noqa: F821
        except Exception:
            exc = np.inf
        evaluated += 1
        if np.isfinite(exc):
            best_list = lst
            best_score = exc
            print(f"[exact] evaluated fast={sc:.10f} exact={exc:.10f} n={len(lst)}", flush=True)

    # Final safety: ensure non-empty, finite, non-negative list.
    try:
        fixed = []
        for x in best_list:
            y = float(x)
            if not np.isfinite(y):
                y = 0.0
            if y < 0.0:
                y = 0.0
            if y > 1000.0:
                y = 1000.0
            fixed.append(y)
        if not fixed or all(v == 0.0 for v in fixed):
            fb = _make_binomial(1024)
            return [float(x) for x in fb / fb.sum()]
        S = sum(fixed)
        if S <= 0.01:
            for i, v in enumerate(fixed):
                fixed[i] = v * (1.0 / S) if S > 0 else 1.0
            S = sum(fixed)
            if S <= 0.01:
                fb = _make_binomial(1024)
                return [float(x) for x in fb / fb.sum()]
        return fixed
    except Exception:
        fb = _make_binomial(1024)
        return [float(x) for x in fb / fb.sum()]
```
